[el_686884ff46ec4495] document="Meridian_Operations_Test.pdf" PDF page 2 section="2.2 Interpretation rules" type=text
First-pass yield measures units accepted without rework as a share of all inspected units. A higher
yield indicates fewer units need rework or rejection. Table 1 does not supply the inspected-unit totals,
so the absolute number of reworked units cannot be calculated from this report.

[el_1bee83426b33486c] document="Meridian_Operations_Test.pdf" PDF page 2 section="relative percent change" type=text
